  unified target, valid XML, idempotent.

v0.2.94
- AF3 SEARCH result tiles now render (movies + tv). Root cause found by
  dumping the skin's runtime-generated includes from the device: each
  search result ROW gates its visibility on
    String.IsEqual(Container(601).ListItem.Property(guid), <row guid>)
  but AF3's script.skinvariables generator (node.py assign_guid) assigns a
  RANDOM guid (f'guid-{random:08x}') to any node item lacking one, and it
  generates the selector buttons (container 601) and the result rows in
  SEPARATE passes. Since we rewrite the searchwidgets node every boot, the
  selector button and its row got DIFFERENT random guids -- e.g. row wanted
  guid-2a0ba77b while the button carried guid-b7c7a8f6 -- so the focused
  category's guid never matched the row's guid and the result tiles stayed
  permanently invisible (blank), even though POV returned valid Art(poster)
  for every item (verified live via JSON-RPC: 20/20 items had posters).
  Discover worked only because it is hardcoded with the literal guid
  'discover' on both sides.
  Fix: pin a STABLE explicit guid on each searchwidgets node item
  ('pov-search-movies' / 'pov-search-tv'). assign_guid preserves an
  existing item guid, so the selector button and its row now always share
  the same guid and the rows render. Verified against the REAL
  skinvariables generator code: with explicit guids all generation passes
  produce identical per-item guids; without them they diverge (reproduces
  the bug). PATCH_VERSION v14 -> v16 rewrites the node + rebuilds on
  existing devices.

v0.2.93
- Genre icons fixed for real, on BOTH skins (root cause found via live
  JSON-RPC + a full skin-variable trace, not guesswork). POV's
  build_shortcut_folder_list stamps the GENERIC POV-logo as Art(fanart) on
  every folder row, including genre rows:
      listitem.setArt({'icon': icon, 'poster': icon, 'thumb': icon,
                       'fanart': fanart, 'banner': icon})
  AF3's genre rows use the Landscape widget style, whose image variable
  ($VAR[Image_Landscape]) prefers Art(fanart) (resolved early) over the
  per-genre icon (resolved last), so the POV logo always won and the real
  genre icon never showed. FENtastic's landscape view has the same
  fanart-first order -- which is why the icons were blank/POV-logo on BOTH
  skins even though navigator.py already emitted the correct per-genre
  icon path and all 28 PNGs were present and valid on disk (verified live:
  every genre item carried Art(fanart)=pov_fanart.png).
  Fix (pov_genre_icons_patcher v2 -> v3): for GENRE rows only (iconImage
  under 'genres/'), use the genre icon as the fanart too, so whichever art
  key the skin prefers lands on the genre icon. Non-genre rows keep the
  normal POV fanart (guarded). Self-tested against real POV source: stock
  and v2 devices both converge, idempotent, patched navigator.py compiles.
  A device on the old v2 marker auto-upgrades on next boot.

v0.2.86
- Embedded English now sinks to the BOTTOM OF THE ENGLISH GROUP only,
  not below other languages. v0.2.84 inserted it at len(f_result) (very
  end, under French/Spanish/etc); v2 of the insert patcher places it
  right after the last 'English' row instead -- so order is Hebrew,
  English subs, embedded English, then other languages. The patcher now
  also re-patches a device that already had v1 applied (regex tolerates
  the v1 marker comment between `index = 0` and the `if`). Verified vs
  the original AND a v1-patched autosub.py: both match, compile, and the
  runtime test confirms embedded English lands at the bottom of English,
  above other langs.

  heal + genre menu-icon patch to BEFORE _maybe_patch_af3_home(), so the
  rebuild repaints the already-healed rows in the same session.

v0.2.85
- THE reason every DarkSubs source patch "didn't apply": DarkSubs
  (service.subtitles.All_Subs) declares reuselanguageinvoker=true and
  runs autosub.py as a persistent xbmc.service. Its Python interpreter
  loads autosub.py/engine.py/sub_window.py into memory at boot and stays
  alive all session, so rewriting those files on disk + dropping the .pyc
  never takes effect this session (and a restart races and loses). That's
  why the embedded-subtitle demote/insert (and other DarkSubs patches)
  appeared to do nothing.
  * New darksubs_reload: after any DarkSubs source patch actually changes
    a file, disable+enable the DarkSubs addon via JSON-RPC so its
    interpreter is torn down and re-imports the PATCHED source. Debounced
    to once per startup; only cycles when something changed (no churn on
    steady-state boots). Wired so the embedded-insert / embedded-demote /
    subwindow-demote patches trigger it. This is what makes the
    embedded-English-at-the-bottom fix finally take effect.
- AF3 home tile ORDER: grouped the Trakt tiles with their media type --
  movies now go הסרטים שלי / (TMDB) / (Trakt), then shows go הסדרות שלי /
  (TMDB) / (Trakt), instead of all movies+shows TMDB then both Trakt.

v0.2.84
- Round 2 of deep-audit fixes (the previous demote/genre/tile attempts
  didn't take on-device; found the true causes):
  * EMBEDDED ENGLISH at the ROOT. DarkSubs autosub.py
    add_embedded_sub_if_exists() computes the embedded-English insert
    index as "right after the last Hebrew subtitle" -- so it lands ABOVE
    the real English subs -- and it does this AFTER engine.sort_subtitles
    runs, which is exactly why both the engine-level and picker-level
    demotes never moved it. New darksubs_embedded_insert_patcher rewrites
    that index to len(f_result) so embedded English is always inserted
    LAST. Verified against the real autosub.py: anchors uniquely on the
    `index = 0` + Hebrew loop (not the earlier settings block), compiles
    (spaces preserved, no TabError), idempotent. Kept the engine + picker
    demotes as belt-and-braces.
  * עידן פלוס "No Results": its AF3 tile had path RunAddon(...), a
    COMMAND, which a CONTENT widget can't list. Changed to
    plugin://plugin.video.idanplus/ (its browsable root) + target=videos,
    so the row shows idanplus's own categories.
  * Trakt tab ADDED to AF3 home: there was never a Trakt tile -- only
    TMDB + POV-local. Added 'הסרטים שלי (Trakt)' and 'הסדרות שלי (Trakt)'
    (action=trakt_collection, build_movie_list/build_tvshow_list) next to
    the TMDB ones.
  * Genre icons on AF3: the v0.2.83 heal is correct but only surfaces
    after a skin rebuild; PATCH_VERSION bumped (v11->v12) so the AF3 home
    rebuilds and re-queries the healed navigator.db rows.

v0.2.83
- Deep-audit fixes for AF3 home + embedded subs (after video/screenshots):
  * EMBEDDED ENGLISH demote that actually works. The engine-level v2
    demote didn't move it on-device. DarkSubs's picker (sub_window.py)
    renders the list in the order it receives and never re-sorts, so we
    now demote at the LAST point -- new darksubs_subwindow_demote_patcher
    injects a reorder right after self.list.reset() in
    set_active_controls that sinks any [LOC] embedded row whose label is
    English ('english'/'eng'/'אנגלית'), permuting BOTH the display list
    (list_o) AND the parallel download list (full_list) in lockstep so
    the pick still downloads the right sub. Genuine embedded Hebrew stays
    on top. Self-tested incl. the lockstep guarantee.
  * Genre icons: UNDO the v0.2.81/0.2.82 navigator.db rewrite, which was
    the cause, not the cure. POV's build_shortcut_folder_list
    (navigator.py:446) prepends media_path() to a row's iconImage, so the
    shipped relative 'genres/genre_*.png' already resolved correctly to
    POV's own (shipped) media/genres/ icons. Our patch had rewritten
    those to an ABSOLUTE special:// path, which POV then doubled into a
    broken '.../media/special://...' path -> POV-logo fallback. Now
    _heal_genre_icon() restores any absolute/empty genre iconImage back
    to the working relative 'genres/<file>' by Hebrew name, and
    pov_genre_icons_patcher additionally hardens navigator.py:446 to pass
    absolute special://‌/http/resource:// icons through unchanged so they
    can never be doubled again. Fixes both skins.
  * AF3 new tiles (networks + idanplus) reliably appear. The rebuild was
    gated on _is_af3_active() at boot AND the version marker was written
    even when the rebuild was skipped -- so if AF3 wasn't reported active
    during the startup run, the nodes were written, the marker advanced,
    and every later boot returned 'already_patched' without ever
    rebuilding. Now the marker is persisted ONLY after a rebuild actually
    runs; a missed rebuild is retried next boot. PATCH_VERSION -> v11 so
    everyone rebuilds once.
  * Note on "הסרטים שלי (TMDB)" = No Results: the widget path is correct;
    it's empty because POV's TMDB favorites READ uses the v4 account id
    (tmdb.account_id), which is empty when only the v3 session is
    connected. The POV-local "הסרטים שלי" tile (watched.db) is unaffected.
    A v4-account-id resolver is the follow-up.

v0.2.82
- Genre icons FOR REAL (both skins). The v0.2.81 approach (repaint AF3
  navigator.db rows + ship icons to build_icons/Genres) didn't fix
  FENtastic, because FENtastic opens genres via mode=navigator.genres --
  a LIVE POV menu that ignores navigator.db. Real root cause: POV's
  menus/navigator.py genres()/anime_genres() hard-code the single generic
  'genres.png' for every genre in the _add_item call, even though POV's
  own meta_lists.py already stores a per-genre icon in value[1]
  (e.g. ['28','genre_action.png']) and ships those PNGs in
  media/genres/. The icon was there; the menu never used it.
  * New pov_genre_icons_patcher rewrites the two per-genre _add_item
    calls from 'genres.png' to 'genres/%s' % value[1], so each genre gets
    its own icon. Both FENtastic and AF3 route through navigator.genres,
    so this fixes BOTH. Also installs our line-art genre PNGs into POV's
    media/genres/ (and adds the one missing stock icon, genre_tv.png).
    Marker-gated, idempotent, .pyc dropped; verified: matches exactly the
    2 genre calls, patched navigator.py compiles, idempotent.

v0.2.81
- Skin polish batch (AF3 + genre icons + embedded-sub ordering):
  * Embedded "תרגום מובנה אנגלית" no longer sits above real English

v0.2.80
- ROOT CAUSE FOUND + FIXED. The instrumented outer except (v0.2.79)
  finally logged it on the device:
    POV_RUN_ERROR: tmdb_favorites() takes 2 positional arguments but 3
      were given | action='tmdb_favorites'
    POV_RUN_ERROR: trakt_collection() takes 2 ... but 3 were given
    POV_RUN_ERROR: get_favorites() takes 3 ... but 4 were given
  Our pov_menus_patcher force-syncs movies.py/tvshows.py onto the device,
  but POV auto-updates its OWN indexers/caches independently. The device
  ended up with a NEWER POV core whose read functions take fewer args
  (the trailing `letter` was dropped) than our synced movies.py passes ->
  every personal list (TMDB/Trakt/POV favorites, movies AND shows) raised
  TypeError in run() and came back empty. "Continue Watching" survived
  only because get_in_progress_items(watched_info, mediatype, *args) has
  *args that swallows the extra arg.
  Fix: new _flex_call(function, *args) wrapper in movies.py/tvshows.py
  (and episodes.py for marker consistency). It calls the read function
  with the full args and, ONLY on a "takes N positional arguments but M
  were given" TypeError, retries with one fewer trailing arg until it
  fits -- so it works against ANY POV arity, present or future. All
  personal-list call sites (tmdb_personal/trakt_personal/mdblist_personal/
  personal_dict) now go through it. Unrelated TypeErrors still propagate.
  pov_menus_patcher freshness MARKER bumped to `_flex_call` so existing
  installs re-sync the fixed menu files once.

v0.2.79
- WIDEN the instrumentation: v0.2.78 logged the per-item build except,
  but the on-device log showed NO POV_BUILD_ITEM_ERROR at all -- the
  favorites list still came back empty in ~213ms. Crucial new clue from
  the user: the "Continue Watching" (in_progress_movies) list WORKS,
  while favorites/tmdb/trakt are all empty. Both go through the identical
  build path, so the failure is OUTSIDE build_movie_content -- in run()'s
  OUTER `except: pass` (menus/movies.py:319 / tvshows.py): the import of
  the read function, the function() call/list-comp, or build_*_results'
  unguarded `self.items.sort(key=int(getProperty('pov_sort_order')))` at
  movies.py:189 (which raises if any item lacks a numeric sort prop, and
  zeroes the WHOLE list).
  * pov_build_content_logger_patcher v2 now instruments BOTH excepts:
    the per-item one (POV_BUILD_ITEM_ERROR) AND run()'s outer one
    (POV_RUN_ERROR, logging action + list length). One of those two will
    fire on the next favorites open and name the exact failure.
  Same catch+skip behaviour, logging only. Both patched files
  py_compile-verified (offline + in-zip).

v0.2.78
- INSTRUMENT the swallowed error that empties favorites lists. v4 proved
  every upstream piece is fine: auth valid, live GET /3/movie/<fav>=200,
  watched.db path correct with 6 rows, get_favorites returns all 6, and
  movie_meta(<fav>) returns a FULL meta dict (all keys, blank_entry
  False) with director/genre/writer/country/duration all valid. So
  build_movie_content SHOULD succeed -- yet the live list is empty in
  ~218ms. That means it raises in the LIVE Kodi context (a listitem op
  we can't simulate offline: make_listitem/setArt/getVideoInfoTag/
  setCast/setCountries/setRating...) and the error is eaten by the bare
  `except: pass` at menus/movies.py:174 / menus/tvshows.py:183.
  * New pov_build_content_logger_patcher rewrites those two bare
    excepts into `except Exception as e: logger("POV_BUILD_ITEM_ERROR",
    repr(e)+tag)` -- same catch+skip behaviour, but the real exception
    (type, message, the tmdb_id it died on) now lands in kodi.log on the
    next favorites open. That single line identifies the bug exactly.
  Marker-gated, idempotent, atomic, .pyc dropped; both patched files
  py_compile-verified.

v0.2.77
- DIAGNOSTIC v4 (final): v3 proved the per-item TMDB fetch SUCCEEDS
  (HTTP 200, "The Mummy"), tmdb_read_token is valid, the watched.db path
  is correct with 6 rows, and get_favorites returns all 6 -- yet the
  list still renders empty in ~218ms with ZERO network calls. The only
  remaining explanation: meta is served from metacache.db and POV's
  per-item BUILD (menus/movies.py build_movie_content) throws on a
  missing/None meta key and is swallowed by its bare `except: pass`
  (movies.py:174), dropping every item silently. v4 replays POV's REAL
  movie_meta() + the exact meta-dependent ops (director/genre/writer
  .split, country, duration int()) for one favorite and prints which one
  raises + the traceback. That line IS the bug. Read-only, silent
  (writes to log + POV_FAV_DIAGNOSTIC.txt).

v0.2.76
- DIAGNOSTIC v3 (decisive): the v0.2.75 fix ran on-device (log confirms
  "blank_entry purge ran: deleted 0" + "widened timeout") yet lists are
  still empty -- which DISPROVES the poisoned-cache theory (there were 0
  blank rows) and the timeout theory (favorites_movies returned in
  ~218ms with ZERO network calls). Offline replay of get_favorites
  against the user's real watched.db returns all 6 rows, so the read
  layer is fine too. Three unknowns remain and v3 checks all three on
  the device, once (silent -- written to log + POV_FAV_DIAGNOSTIC.txt,
  no popup):
  * tmdb_read_token presence (the token movie_details/tvshow_details
    actually use for per-item meta -- never inspected before; empty =>
    every favorite resolves blank while popular lists still work since
    they use tmdb.token).
  * the EXACT watched.db path POV resolves + its movie-row count (in
    case adds land in one db and the list reads another/empty one).
  * a LIVE GET /3/movie/<fav id> with Bearer <tmdb_read_token> -> prints
    HTTP status (200 success vs 401 unauthorized vs timeout). This single
    call definitively separates token/fetch failure from a render bug.
  No behaviour change to favorites; read-only.

v0.2.75
- ACTUALLY apply the favorites fix (v0.2.74 shipped the right idea but
  didn't take effect on devices). On-device proof: watched.db held 6
  valid favorite rows, yet the POV-local favorites list returned in
  212ms with ZERO network calls and rendered empty -- i.e. all 6 ids
  were served from the per-item meta cache as blank_entry and dropped,
  and kodi.log showed the patcher never logged at all. Root problem:
  v0.2.74 tied the poisoned-row purge to the SUCCESS of rewriting
  metadata.py, so if that rewrite path was skipped the purge never ran
  and the cache stayed poisoned forever.
  * pov_meta_blank_patcher v2: the metacache.db blank_entry purge now
    runs UNCONDITIONALLY and FIRST on every startup, independent of the
    metadata.py rewrite, and ALWAYS logs how many rows it deleted (v1's
    silence is what hid the skip). This makes existing favorites
    reappear immediately.
  * Also widen tmdb_api's aggressive per-item timeout 3.05s -> 15.05s --
    the actual reason a /movie/{id} detail fetch failed and got cached
    blank in the first place on mobile/slow links. With the wider
    timeout the re-fetch succeeds and posters fill in instead of
    re-poisoning.

v0.2.74
- FIX the real "favorites added but list shows 0" bug (root cause found
  via the v0.2.72/0.2.73 diagnostic). The diagnostic proved on-device:
  the favorite rows ARE saved (watched.db -> favorites) and TMDB/Trakt
  auth is fully valid -- yet both the POV-local and TMDB favorites tiles
  showed 0, in BOTH skins, while normal browsing worked. Root cause was
  the PER-ITEM metadata cache: POV's movie_meta/tvshow_meta persist a
  {'blank_entry': True} into metacache.db for 2 DAYS whenever a single
  movie_details() fetch fails transiently (3.05s timeout / blip), and
  menus drop any item whose meta is blank_entry. So one hiccup hid a
  favorite for ~48h regardless of skin or tile. This was the one cache
  neither pov_cache_empty_patcher (maincache.db) nor
  pov_trakt_cache_empty_patcher (trakt.db) covered.
  * New pov_meta_blank_patcher: stops movie_meta/tvshow_meta from
    persisting transient blank_entry (mirrors the "never cache a
    failure" approach of the two sibling cache patchers), and one-shot-
    clears any rows already poisoned with blank_entry from metacache.db
    so existing favorites recover immediately instead of waiting 2 days.
- AF3 home: show BOTH a POV-local favorites tile ("הסרטים שלי" ->
  favorites_movies/favorites_tvshows, the store the in-app add writes
  to, so it populates instantly) AND the online TMDB.org tile
  ("...(TMDB)" -> tmdb_favorites), side by side, for movies and shows.
  Bumped AF3 home PATCH_VERSION so existing installs rebuild the
  shortcuts and pick up the new tiles.

v0.2.73
- Make the favorites diagnostic SILENT: the v0.2.72 diagnostic popped a
  full-screen textviewer on every device that applied it, which is fine
  for one debugging session but annoying as a general rollout. The
  popup is removed; the diagnostic still writes its report to kodi.log
  and to <POV profile>/POV_FAV_DIAGNOSTIC.txt so we can still read the
  on-device state when needed, with zero on-screen interruption. No
  behaviour change to favorites themselves.

v0.2.72
- DIAGNOSTIC build (no behaviour change to favorites): the user still
  sees "Add to list" report success but "My Movies"/"My Shows" stay at
  0 results even after leaving and re-entering -- which means the READ
  genuinely returns empty, not a refresh problem. Rather than guess
  again, this ships a one-shot, read-only diagnostic
  (pov_favorites_diagnostic) that dumps the real on-device state:
    * TMDB auth: tmdb.account_id (v4 READ id) vs tmdb.session_account_id
      (v3 WRITE id) -- if account_id is empty the favorites read URL
      breaks and POV returns [] forever regardless of cache. Tokens are
      shown as presence/length only, never in full.
    * Trakt auth: trakt_user / token / expires presence.
    * POV-local favorites DB (watched.db -> favorites): row counts per
      type + a small sample, to see if items actually saved and whether
      their tmdb_id looks valid (vs null/empty).
    * TMDB list cache (maincache.db) + Trakt cache (traktcache.db):
      which list ids are cached and whether they're empty/near-empty.
  Output goes to kodi.log, to <POV profile>/POV_FAV_DIAGNOSTIC.txt, and
  to a textviewer dialog popped once so it can be screenshotted. Gated
  to run once. Nothing in POV is modified.

v0.2.71
- HOTFIX for v0.2.70: adding to a list crashed Kodi. v0.2.70's
  pov_favorites_refresh_patcher, besides the (correct) container_refresh()
  on add, also called POV's kodi_utils.widget_refresh() to reload the
  home-screen widget tiles. That refresh uses UpdateLibrary(video,
  special://skin/foo) -- the same trick POV itself only ever runs from
  a background thread, gated behind an opt-in setting, never from a
  context-menu action -- and it crashed Kodi every time the user hit
  "Add to list".
  v2 of the patcher removes the widget_refresh() call entirely and keeps
  ONLY container_refresh(), which is exactly what POV already does
  safely on remove (just now extended to add). New marker
  AI_SUBS_FAV_REFRESH_v2; the patcher heals installs that already got
  the crashing v1 line (strips the widget_refresh() off all three sites)
  as well as pristine POV. Verified: pristine->v2 and v1->v2 both leave
  no kodi_utils.widget_refresh() call, 3 sites patched, result parses,
  idempotent.
  Trade-off: the home-screen widget tile now refreshes on the next
  return to the home screen (the list cache is still busted on add)
  rather than instantly -- a small delay in exchange for not crashing.
  The in-list refresh (open "My Movies"/"My Shows" -> add -> appears
  immediately) is unchanged and still works.

v0.2.70
- Follow-up to #187/#188 (the "Add to My List" saga): those fixed the
  READ side -- POV was caching empty list results (main_cache.py 24h,
  trakt_cache.py forever) so a just-added title never showed. With the
  caches no longer stuck-empty, the remaining gap is the WRITE side:
  POV's modules/dialogs.py only refreshes the screen after a REMOVE,
  never after an ADD. The container-refresh call is gated:
    - tmdb_manager_choice (favorites/watchlist): if not action: container_refresh()
    - tmdb_manager_choice (custom list):         if not action: container_refresh()
    - favorites_choice    (POV-local):           if refresh: container_refresh()
  On add, action/refresh is truthy/False, so nothing refreshes -- you
  get the "added" toast but the open "My Movies"/"My Shows" list (and
  the home widget tile) keeps showing the old contents until you leave
  and come back. Removing already refreshed instantly, which is why the
  asymmetry looked like "adding is broken".
  New pov_favorites_refresh_patcher drops the gate so the refresh fires
  on add as well as remove, and additionally calls POV's own
  kodi_utils.widget_refresh() so the home-screen widget tiles reload too
  (Container.Refresh alone only reloads an open list, not a home widget;
  POV uses widget_refresh() the same way in entry.py after a Trakt sync).
  Three context-anchored replacements, marker-gated (AI_SUBS_FAV_REFRESH),
  idempotent, atomic write, no-op on installs without POV. Verified
  against the real bundled POV dialogs.py (3/3 anchors matched, result
  parses). Covers the TMDB Favorites/Watchlist, custom-list, and
  POV-local favorites paths.

v0.2.69
- Follow-up fix: POV's TRAKT cache is in a separate DB
  (trakt.db) and uses a separate function (cache_trakt_object) that
  v0.2.68 did NOT patch. Critically, that cache has NO expiration --
  empty results cached there stay forever until an explicit
  clear_trakt_collection_watchlist_data() call. End-user report: the
  "My Movies (Trakt)" tile stayed empty even after v0.2.68 landed,
  because the trakt.db row was still poisoned with a stuck empty.
  New pov_trakt_cache_empty_patcher mirrors v0.2.68's main_cache fix
  for trakt_cache.py: inserts `if not result: return result` before
  the `dbcur.execute(TC_BASE_SET, ...)` line so empty results are
  no longer persisted, and one-shot-clears any
  trakt_collection_* / trakt_watchlist_* / trakt_favorites_* /
  trakt_user_lists* / trakt_lists_* rows currently sitting in
  trakt.db so existing victims don't have to wait for an explicit
  clear. Marker-gated (AI_SUBS_POV_TRAKT_CACHE_EMPTY_v1),
  idempotent, no-op on installs without POV.

v0.2.68
- Two fixes for the "Add to My List" buttons that stopped working
  in the latest updates:

  1) favourites_personal_tiles_patcher (new): restores the 6 personal
     home tiles ("הסרטים שלי / הסדרות שלי" in TMDB / Trakt / POV
     variants) when they're missing from userdata/favourites.xml.
     Root cause: the wizard's per-skin seed at
     media/builds_favourites_xml/skin.fentastic/favourites.xml only
     contains 11 service tiles, while the build's install default
     ships 32. When the user switches skin to AF3 and back to
     FENtastic, the wizard's update_favourites_xml_file() copies the
     stripped 11-tile seed over the working 32-tile favourites.xml,
     wiping every content tile including the 6 personal ones. The
     patcher detects the missing tiles by name substring and
     restores them from a bundled canonical fixture.

  2) pov_cache_empty_patcher (new): inserts `if not result: return
     result` into plugin.video.pov/.../caches/main_cache.py's
     cache_object(). POV's pattern of caching API results for 24h
     unconditionally meant that any transient TMDB/Trakt list-read
     failure (returning empty by the defensive `except: return []`
     in _get_tmdblist_paginated_list and similar) would cache the
     empty list for a full 24h. End-user symptom: add an item via
     "ניהול רשימות (TMDB)" -> success notification -> "הסרטים שלי
     (TMDB)" home tile shows "No results" until cache expires.
     User confirmed items DO get added on themoviedb.org / trakt.tv
     -- the write works, just the read returns the stuck-empty cache.
     With this patch empty results are never persisted; next tile open
     re-fetches. Also one-shot clears any tmdblist_* / trakt_* rows
     already sitting empty in maincache.db so existing victims don't
     need to wait out the 24h.

  Both patchers are marker-gated, idempotent, atomic writes, no-op
  on installs without the affected files. Smoke-tested against the
  real user's favourites.xml shape (4 cases) and the real POV
  main_cache.py shape (5 cases).

v0.2.67
- Actually fix the jurialmunkey upgrade (0.2.66 didn't take). The wizard's
  AF3 version gate read the XML declaration's version="1.0" instead of the
  addon's real version, so every installed addon looked like "1.0" and
  1.0 >= 0.2.35 passed -> the deps pack was still skipped and jurialmunkey
  stayed at 0.2.28. Requires wizard 0.1.14 (shipped in the same quickfix).

v0.2.66
- Fix AF3 TMDbHelper crash ("No module named 'jurialmunkey.ftools'"):
  AF3's bundled TMDbHelper 6.15.6 needs script.module.jurialmunkey >= 0.2.35,
  but users who switched to AF3 with an older jurialmunkey (e.g. 0.2.28) kept
  the stale copy -- the deps pack was skipped because it only checked the file
  EXISTED, not its version. TMDbHelper's service then crash-looped on every
  startup and AF3 widgets/ratings broke. Now: the AF3 home patcher detects an
  old jurialmunkey and re-triggers the deps install, and the wizard's deps
  pack gained a version gate (>= 0.2.35) so the re-extract actually overwrites
  the stale module instead of skipping it.

v0.2.65
- Fix: the embedded-demote re-sort is now scoped to the English group
  ONLY. The 0.2.64 cut re-sorted the combined list by site_id '[LOC]',
  which DarkSubs also stamps on the embedded HEBREW entry -- that would
  have sunk native Hebrew embedded subs below English. Hebrew (embedded
  or not) is now never touched and stays first, as it must.
- Shipped via quickfix so it reaches existing installs without a full
  build reinstall.

v0.2.64
- Embedded ENGLISH ("[LOC]") subtitles now sink to the bottom of the
  English group in the DarkSubs picker instead of floating to the top on
  their hard-coded 101% sync. On this streaming build the embedded track
  can't be AI-translated (DarkSubs short-circuits embedded picks with
  setSubtitleStream before the AI hook runs), so demoting it makes the
  external, AI-translatable English source the natural first pick.
  Embedded HEBREW is deliberately left untouched -- it stays first, as
  it should (native Hebrew, perfectly synced, no translation needed).
  New self-healing patcher (AI_EMBEDDED_DEMOTE_v1), scoped to the English
  group only; no-op when DarkSubs isn't installed or engine.py changed.

v0.2.63
- AF3 phone/touch pass (iteration 1): enable Kodi's mouse/pointer support
  so the real home controls (widget rows, spotlight, the submenu buttons
  once open) respond to taps and lists can be drag-scrolled on a phone.
  AF3's main menu items are off-screen "fake-focus" controls, so a deeper
  overlay fix is still planned - this is the safe first step.

v0.2.62
- New self-healing patcher all_subs_samefile_patcher: wraps the 6
  shutil.copy() calls in service.subtitles.all_subs_plus's
  setLanguageSettings() in try/except shutil.SameFileError. On
  Windows (NTFS junctions/hardlinks) the unpatched AllSubs raises
  SameFileError at module-load time when source and destination
  language .po files resolve to the same inode, killing autosub.py
  before Kodi even shows the home screen -- user-visible Python
  error every PC boot, AllSubs functionality fully broken. Patch
  is idempotent, marker-gated (AI_SUBS_ALL_SUBS_SAMEFILE_v1), and
  no-op on installs without AllSubs.

  instead of the Combined category selector, because the selector is
  awkward and can mis-target taps on phones.
- Adds a real "כלים וחיבורים" widget row backed by the wizard. This
  makes Connect Services, POV, AI settings, Quick Update, Switch Skin,

v0.2.53
- Wizard self-healer v3: APK-override. Real-user log after v2
  ship showed zero healer log lines AND wizard staying at 0.1.4
  -- root cause: on the Android APK build the wizard ships
  inside the APK at /data/.../cache/apk/assets/addons/, which
  is read-only; the writable mirror at special://home/addons/
  may not exist at all. v2 checked the writable dir, found
  nothing, returned 'no_wizard' silently and did nothing.
  v3 fix: don't require the writable dir to pre-exist. If
  xbmcaddon.Addon('plugin.program.kodipovilwizard') resolves
  (which it does for APK-bundled installs), we extract the
  staged zip into the writable path -- creating the directory
  if needed. Kodi's normal addon-resolution picks the higher-
  versioned writable copy over the APK's 0.1.4 (proven in the
  same user's log via skin.fentastic: APK ships its own copy
  but Kodi loads writable v1.0.25). Also: every return code
  now logs at INFO (was silent on 'quiet steady-state' return
  codes in v2 -- absence-of-logs vs absence-of-bug is invisible
  to remote diagnosis). Marker bumped to v3 so v2-stuck installs
  re-try with v3's correct path.
v0.2.52
- Wizard self-healer v2: bundled instead of downloaded. User
  report after v1 ship: no toast appeared after quick_update +
  restart -- the v1 healer was silently failing (most likely an
  SSL/urllib edge case on the user's Android Kodi build). v2 ships
  the wizard zip *inside* this addon (resources/staged_wizard.zip)
  and the healer reads from disk. No network, no SSL, no CDN. Also
  swapped the success-path notification from a transient toast
  (8s) to a modal Dialog().ok the user must acknowledge -- prevents
  the "didn't see the message, didn't know to restart" failure
  mode. Failure paths now toast Hebrew error text so users have
  signal when something can't complete (was completely silent in
  v1). Adds best-effort __pycache__ cleanup so Python picks up the
  new .py files cleanly on next boot. Marker bumped to v2 so
  installs that ran v1 (and got nothing) re-try with v2's path.
v0.2.51
- Wizard self-healer (new module wizard_self_healer.py) rides this
  addon's quickfix path to recover users stuck on a pre-0.1.10
  wizard. Root cause: the wizard's quick_update extract.all
  silently skips every file whose path contains the wizard's own
  addon id (a safety guard against the wizard trashing itself
  mid-extract), so every wizard update shipped via quickfix was
  dropped on the floor. End-user symptom: after PR #161 (Arctic
  Fuse 3 third skin) + PR #162 (quickfix-bundles-wizard) +
  quick_update + restart, Switch Skin still listed only Estuary
  and FENtastic. Healer fingerprints the installed wizard.py for a
  0.1.10-or-newer sentinel; if absent, downloads the latest wizard
  zip from GitHub Pages and force-writes the files into the
  wizard's addon dir (different addon id = not skipped by anyone).
  Toasts the user to restart so Python re-imports the new wizard.
  Marker-gated, no-op on healthy wizards, no-op on AI-only
  installs (no wizard addon present).
v0.2.50
- Arctic Fuse 3 subtitle-picker header now prefers our
  subs.player_filename window property over Player.FileName (fixes
  the TorBox CDN UUID-as-title regression that AF3 users would
  have seen — same fix we shipped for FENtastic via
  skin_dialog_subtitles_patcher, but adapted to AF3's structure).
  Dedicated patcher af3_dialog_subtitles_patcher targets
  skin.arctic.fuse.3/1080i/Dialog_DialogSubtitles.xml directly.
  AF3 puts the header label in a <param> inside an include block
  (not a <control type="label"> the generic patcher matches), so
  this needs its own regex + injection logic. Solution: inject a
  conditional <variable name="ai_subs_header_label"> at the top of
  the file's <includes> root (priority: our window property; fallback:
  Player.FileName), then swap the param value to $VAR[...]. Both
  edits in the same file, atomic write, marker-gated, idempotent.
  No-op when AF3 isn't installed -- self-heals on the next Kodi

- Arctic Fuse 3 first-launch dialog: when the user activates the
